#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
}

void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
        }
    }
}

void matrixMultiply(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B, 
                    std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
            double sum = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                sum += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            C[i * N + j] = sum;
        }
    }
}

// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
    // Check a few random positions
    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
    
    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
            
            double expected = 0.0;
            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
            }
            
            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
            
            if (relError > 1e-6) {
                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t N = 512;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            N = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
    printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    // Allocate matrices
    std::vector<double> A(N * N);
    std::vector<double> B(N * N);
    std::vector<double> C(N * N);
    
    // Initialize matrices
    printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
    initMatrix(A, N);
    initMatrix(B, N);
    
    // Perform matrix multiplication
    printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    matrixMultiply(A, B, C, N);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate GFLOPS
    double gflops = (2.0 * N * N * N) / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(C, "MatrixC");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)

+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
+            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)

+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

The implementation now uses MPI row decomposition, OpenMP host work, and per-rank CUDA/cuBLAS GEMM; next I’m compiling and exercising single- and multi-rank correctness paths.

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + localRow;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            matrix[localRow * N + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);

     }

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
+            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + localRow;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            matrix[localRow * N + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);

     }

+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
+            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

-// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows; ++localRow) {

+const char* cublasStatusName(const cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
+            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)

+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {
-    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}

+void printUsage(const char* programName) {

+    std::printf("Options:\n");

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

-constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
+#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
+    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows; ++localRow) {
+        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + localRow;
+        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
+            matrix[localRow * N + column] =
+                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
         }
     }

+const char* cublasStatusName(const cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
+            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    std::printf("Options:\n");

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};

    20	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
    21	// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
    22	// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
    23	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
    24	                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
    25	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
    26	           static_cast<double>(N * N);
    27	}
    28	
    29	struct RowRange {
    30	    size_t first;
    31	    size_t count;
    32	};
    33	
    34	RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
    35	    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
    36	    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
    37	    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
    38	    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
    39	            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
    40	}
    41	
    42	void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
    43	                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {
    44	#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)
    45	    for (size_t localRow = 0; localRow < rows; ++localRow) {
    46	        const size_t globalRow = firstRow + localRow;
    47	        for (size_t column = 0; column < N; ++column) {
    48	            matrix[localRow * N + column] =
    49	                getPseudoRndValue(N, globalRow, column);
    50	        }
    51	    }
    52	}
    53	
    54	const char* cublasStatusName(const cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
    55	    switch (status) {
    56	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS:
    57	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS";
    58	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED:
    59	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED";
    60	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED:
    61	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED";
    62	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE:
    63	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE";
    64	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH:
    65	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH";
    66	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR:
    67	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR";
    68	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED:
    69	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED";
    70	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR:
    71	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR";
    72	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED:
    73	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED";
    74	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR:
    75	            return "CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR";
    76	        default:
    77	            return "unknown cuBLAS status";
    78	    }
    79	}

    81	[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
    82	                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
    83	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
    84	                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
    85	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    86	    std::abort();
    87	}
    88	
    89	[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
    90	                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
    91	    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
    92	                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
    93	    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
    94	    std::abort();
    95	}

    97	#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
    98	    do {                                                                          \
    99	        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
   100	        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
   101	            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
   102	        }                                                                         \
   103	    } while (false)
   104	
   105	#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
   106	    do {                                                                          \
   107	        const cublasStatus_t cublasCheckResult = (call);                          \
   108	        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \
   109	            abortCublas(#call, cublasCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));    \
   110	        }                                                                         \
   111	    } while (false)

   113	struct DeviceResources {
   114	    double* A = nullptr;
   115	    double* B = nullptr;
   116	    double* C = nullptr;
   117	    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   118	    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
   119	};
   120	
   121	void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
   122	    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
   123	        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
   124	    }
   125	    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
   126	        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
   127	    }
   128	    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
   129	        cudaFree(resources.C);
   130	    }
   131	    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
   132	        cudaFree(resources.B);
   133	    }
   134	    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
   135	        cudaFree(resources.A);
   136	    }
   137	}
   138	
   139	// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
   140	// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
   141	void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
   142	                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
   143	    if (localRows == 0) {
   144	        return;
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
   148	    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
   149	    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
   150	    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
   151	    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   152	                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
   153	                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
   154	                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
   155	                 rank);
   156	}
   157	
   158	bool validateLocalResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, const size_t N,
   159	                         const RowRange rows, const int rank) {
   160	    constexpr size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1, 2, 3, 4};
   161	
   162	    for (const size_t rawI : checkPoints) {
   163	        const size_t i = rawI % N;
   164	        if (i < rows.first || i >= rows.first + rows.count) {
   165	            continue;
   166	        }
   167	        for (const size_t rawJ : checkPoints) {
   168	            const size_t j = rawJ % N;
   169	            double expected = 0.0;
   170	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   171	                expected += getPseudoRndValue(N, i, k) *
   172	                            getPseudoRndValue(N, k, j);
   173	            }
   174	
   175	            const double actual = localC[(i - rows.first) * N + j];
   176	            const double relativeError =
   177	                std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   178	            if (relativeError > 1e-6) {
   179	                std::fprintf(stderr,
   180	                             "Rank %d: validation failed at (%zu, %zu): "
   181	                             "expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   182	                             rank, i, j, expected, actual, relativeError);
   183	                return false;
   184	            }
   185	        }
   186	    }
   187	    return true;
   188	}
   189	
   190	// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
   191	// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
   192	void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
   193	                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
   194	    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
   195	    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
   196	        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
   197	
   198	    if (rank == 0) {
   199	        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
   200	        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
   201	                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
   202	
   203	        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
   204	            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
   205	            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
   206	            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
   207	            while (remaining != 0) {
   208	                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
   209	                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
   210	                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
   211	                offset += chunk;
   212	                remaining -= chunk;
   213	            }
   214	        }
   215	    } else {
   216	        size_t remaining = localC.size();
   217	        size_t offset = 0;
   218	        while (remaining != 0) {
   219	            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
   220	            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
   221	                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   222	            offset += chunk;
   223	            remaining -= chunk;
   224	        }
   225	    }
   226	}
   227	
   228	void printUsage(const char* programName) {
   229	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", programName);
   230	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   231	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Matrix size N (computes NxN * NxN) (default: 512)\n");
   232	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   233	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   234	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   235	}
   236	
   237	bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
   238	    errno = 0;
   239	    char* end = nullptr;
   240	    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   241	    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
   242	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
   243	        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
   244	        return false;
   245	    }
   246	    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
   247	    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
   248	           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
   249	}
   250	
   251	}  // namespace
   252	
   253	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   254	    int providedThreadLevel = MPI_THREAD_SINGLE;
   255	    if (MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED,
   256	                        &providedThreadLevel) != MPI_SUCCESS) {
   257	        std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI initialization failed\n");
   258	        return EXIT_FAILURE;
   259	    }
   260	
   261	    int rank = 0;
   262	    int ranks = 1;
   263	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   264	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
   265	    if (providedThreadLevel < MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED) {
   266	        if (rank == 0) {
   267	            std::fprintf(stderr, "MPI does not provide required thread support\n");
   268	        }
   269	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   270	    }
   271	
   272	    size_t N = 512;
   273	    bool validate = false;
   274	    bool printResults = false;
   275	    bool showHelp = false;
   276	    bool argumentsValid = true;
   277	
   278	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   279	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   280	            if (!parseSize(argv[++i], N)) {
   281	                argumentsValid = false;
   282	            }
   283	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   284	            validate = true;
   285	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   286	            printResults = true;
   287	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   288	            showHelp = true;
   289	        } else {
   290	            if (rank == 0) {
   291	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   292	            }
   293	            argumentsValid = false;
   294	        }
   295	    }
   296	
   297	    if (showHelp || !argumentsValid) {
   298	        if (rank == 0) {
   299	            if (!argumentsValid) {
   300	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Matrix size must be a positive, supported integer.\n");
   301	            }
   302	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   303	        }
   304	        MPI_Finalize();
   305	        return argumentsValid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   306	    }
   307	
   308	    const RowRange localRows = rowsForRank(N, rank, ranks);
   309	    const size_t fullElements = N * N;
   310	    const size_t localElements = localRows.count * N;
   311	    const size_t fullBytes = fullElements * sizeof(double);
   312	    const size_t localBytes = localElements * sizeof(double);
   313	
   314	    MPI_Comm localCommunicator = MPI_COMM_NULL;
   315	    MPI_Comm_split_type(MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_COMM_TYPE_SHARED, rank, MPI_INFO_NULL,
   316	                        &localCommunicator);
   317	    int localRank = 0;
   318	    MPI_Comm_rank(localCommunicator, &localRank);
   319	
   320	    int deviceCount = 0;
   321	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetDeviceCount(&deviceCount), rank);
   322	    if (deviceCount == 0) {
   323	        if (rank == 0) {
   324	            std::fprintf(stderr, "No CUDA device is visible\n");
   325	        }
   326	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   327	    }
   328	    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;
   329	    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device), rank);
   330	
   331	    int openmpThreads = 1;
   332	#pragma omp parallel
   333	    {
   334	#pragma omp master
   335	        openmpThreads = omp_get_num_threads();
   336	    }
   337	    int minimumOpenmpThreads = 1;
   338	    int maximumOpenmpThreads = 1;
   339	    MPI_Reduce(&openmpThreads, &minimumOpenmpThreads, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, 0,
   340	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   341	    MPI_Reduce(&openmpThreads, &maximumOpenmpThreads, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MAX, 0,
   342	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   343	
   344	    if (rank == 0) {
   345	        std::printf("Matrix Multiplication Benchmark\n");
   346	        std::printf("Matrix size: %zu x %zu\n", N, N);
   347	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   348	        std::printf("Hybrid configuration: %d MPI rank(s), %d-%d OpenMP thread(s) "
   349	                    "per rank, CUDA/cuBLAS\n",
   350	                    ranks, minimumOpenmpThreads, maximumOpenmpThreads);
   351	        std::printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
   352	    }
   353	
   354	    std::vector<double> hostA;
   355	    std::vector<double> hostB;
   356	    try {
   357	        hostA.resize(localElements);
   358	        if (localRows.count != 0) {
   359	            hostB.resize(fullElements);
   360	        }
   361	    } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {
   362	        std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: host matrix allocation failed\n", rank);
   363	        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   364	    }
   365	
   366	    initMatrixRows(hostA, N, localRows.first, localRows.count);
   367	    if (localRows.count != 0) {
   368	        initMatrixRows(hostB, N, 0, N);
   369	    }
   370	
   371	    DeviceResources resources;
   372	    if (localRows.count != 0) {
   373	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&resources.A), localBytes), rank);
   374	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&resources.B), fullBytes), rank);
   375	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&resources.C), localBytes), rank);
   376	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&resources.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
   377	                   rank);
   378	        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasCreate(&resources.handle), rank);
   379	        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasSetStream(resources.handle, resources.stream), rank);
   380	        CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasSetMathMode(resources.handle, CUBLAS_DEFAULT_MATH), rank);
   381	
   382	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.A, hostA.data(), localBytes,
   383	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
   384	                   rank);
   385	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(resources.B, hostB.data(), fullBytes,
   386	                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, resources.stream),
   387	                   rank);
   388	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), rank);
   389	
   390	        // Exclude one-time CUDA context and library initialization from the
   391	        // benchmark, matching the original program's compute-only timing.
   392	        launchGemm(resources, N, localRows.count, rank);
   393	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), rank);
   394	    }
   395	
   396	    if (rank == 0) {
   397	        std::printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
   398	    }
   399	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   400	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   401	    launchGemm(resources, N, localRows.count, rank);
   402	    if (localRows.count != 0) {
   403	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), rank);
   404	    }
   405	    const double localSeconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   406	
   407	    double elapsedSeconds = 0.0;
   408	    MPI_Reduce(&localSeconds, &elapsedSeconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   409	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   410	
   411	    if (rank == 0) {
   412	        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
   413	        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
   414	                                  static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);
   415	        const double gflops = operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9;
   416	        std::printf("Computation time: %lld ms\n", milliseconds);
   417	        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
   418	    }
   419	
   420	    std::vector<double> localC;
   421	    if (validate || printResults) {
   422	        try {
   423	            localC.resize(localElements);
   424	        } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {
   425	            std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: host result allocation failed\n", rank);
   426	            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   427	        }
   428	        if (localRows.count != 0) {
   429	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), resources.C, localBytes,
   430	                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
   431	                       rank);
   432	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), rank);
   433	        }
   434	    }
   435	
   436	    if (printResults) {
   437	        std::vector<double> fullC;
   438	        if (rank == 0) {
   439	            try {
   440	                fullC.resize(fullElements);
   441	            } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {
   442	                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank 0: full result allocation failed\n");
   443	                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
   444	            }
   445	        }
   446	        gatherResult(localC, fullC, N, rank, ranks);
   447	        if (rank == 0) {
   448	            print_results(fullC, "MatrixC");
   449	        }
   450	    }
   451	
   452	    bool globallyValid = true;
   453	    if (validate) {
   454	        if (rank == 0) {
   455	            std::printf("Validating result...\n");
   456	        }
   457	        const int localValid = validateLocalResult(localC, N, localRows, rank) ? 1 : 0;
   458	        int allValid = 0;
   459	        MPI_Allreduce(&localValid, &allValid, 1, MPI_INT, MPI_MIN, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   460	        globallyValid = allValid != 0;
   461	        if (rank == 0) {
   462	            std::printf("Validation: %s\n", globallyValid ? "PASSED" : "FAILED");
   463	        }
   464	    }
   465	
   466	    destroyDeviceResources(resources);
   467	    MPI_Comm_free(&localCommunicator);
   468	    MPI_Finalize();
   469	    return globallyValid ? EXIT_SUCCESS : EXIT_FAILURE;
   470	}

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+void initMatrixRows(std::vector<double>& matrix, const size_t N,
+                    const size_t firstRow, const size_t rows) {

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}

+[[noreturn]] void abortCublas(const char* expression, const cublasStatus_t status,
+                              const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \
+    do {                                                                          \
+        const cudaError_t cudaCheckResult = (call);                               \
+        if (cudaCheckResult != cudaSuccess) {                                     \
+            abortCuda(#call, cudaCheckResult, __FILE__, __LINE__, (rank));        \
+        }                                                                         \
+    } while (false)
+
+#define CUBLAS_CHECK(call, rank)                                                  \
+    do {                                                                          \

+        if (cublasCheckResult != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {                         \

+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}
+
+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}

+    }

+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+        const auto milliseconds = static_cast<long long>(elapsedSeconds * 1000.0);
+        const double operations = 2.0 * static_cast<double>(N) *
+                                  static_cast<double>(N) * static_cast<double>(N);
+        const double gflops = operations / elapsedSeconds / 1.0e9;

+        std::printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);

+        } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {

+            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+        if (localRows.count != 0) {
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(localC.data(), resources.C, localBytes,
+                                       cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, resources.stream),
+                       rank);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaStreamSynchronize(resources.stream), rank);
+        }

+            } catch (const std::bad_alloc&) {
+                std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank 0: full result allocation failed\n");
+                MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+            }
+        }

+namespace {
+
+// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization.  This is kept
+// identical to the serial benchmark so that every rank can independently
+// generate its part of A and the replicated B without communicating them.
+constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i,
+                                   const size_t j) noexcept {
+    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) /
+           static_cast<double>(N * N);
+}
+
+struct RowRange {
+    size_t first;
+    size_t count;
+};
+
+RowRange rowsForRank(const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) noexcept {
+    const size_t base = N / static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t extra = N % static_cast<size_t>(ranks);
+    const size_t rankAsSize = static_cast<size_t>(rank);
+    return {rankAsSize * base + std::min(rankAsSize, extra),
+            base + (rankAsSize < extra ? 1U : 0U)};
 }

+[[noreturn]] void abortCuda(const char* expression, const cudaError_t error,
+                            const char* file, const int line, const int rank) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: CUDA call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,

+    std::fprintf(stderr, "Rank %d: cuBLAS call %s failed at %s:%d: %s\n", rank,
+                 expression, file, line, cublasStatusName(status));
+    MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, EXIT_FAILURE);
+    std::abort();

+#define CUDA_CHECK(call, rank)                                                   \

+struct DeviceResources {
+    double* A = nullptr;
+    double* B = nullptr;
+    double* C = nullptr;
+    cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+    cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+};
+
+void destroyDeviceResources(DeviceResources& resources) noexcept {
+    if (resources.handle != nullptr) {
+        cublasDestroy(resources.handle);
+    }
+    if (resources.stream != nullptr) {
+        cudaStreamDestroy(resources.stream);
+    }
+    if (resources.C != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.C);
+    }
+    if (resources.B != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.B);
+    }
+    if (resources.A != nullptr) {
+        cudaFree(resources.A);
+    }
+}

+// cuBLAS uses column-major matrices.  Row-major A*B is therefore evaluated as
+// C^T = B^T*A^T, which also leaves C in the desired row-major byte layout.
+void launchGemm(DeviceResources& resources, const size_t N,
+                const size_t localRows, const int rank) {
+    if (localRows == 0) {
+        return;
     }
+
+    const int dimension = static_cast<int>(N);
+    const int columnsOfTransposedC = static_cast<int>(localRows);
+    constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+    constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+    CUBLAS_CHECK(cublasDgemm(resources.handle, CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                            dimension, columnsOfTransposedC, dimension, &alpha,
+                            resources.B, dimension, resources.A, dimension,
+                            &beta, resources.C, dimension),
+                 rank);
 }

-void printUsage(const char* progName) {

+// MPI-3 collective counts are int-sized.  Chunked point-to-point transfers
+// retain support for matrices whose total element count exceeds INT_MAX.
+void gatherResult(const std::vector<double>& localC, std::vector<double>& fullC,
+                  const size_t N, const int rank, const int ranks) {
+    constexpr int resultTag = 1729;
+    constexpr size_t maxChunk =
+        static_cast<size_t>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max());
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const RowRange rootRows = rowsForRank(N, 0, ranks);
+        std::copy(localC.begin(), localC.end(),
+                  fullC.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(rootRows.first * N));
+
+        for (int source = 1; source < ranks; ++source) {
+            const RowRange sourceRows = rowsForRank(N, source, ranks);
+            size_t remaining = sourceRows.count * N;
+            size_t offset = sourceRows.first * N;
+            while (remaining != 0) {
+                const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+                MPI_Recv(fullC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                         source, resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD, MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);
+                offset += chunk;
+                remaining -= chunk;
+            }
+        }
+    } else {
+        size_t remaining = localC.size();
+        size_t offset = 0;
+        while (remaining != 0) {
+            const size_t chunk = std::min(remaining, maxChunk);
+            MPI_Send(localC.data() + offset, static_cast<int>(chunk), MPI_DOUBLE, 0,
+                     resultTag, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+            offset += chunk;
+            remaining -= chunk;
+        }
+    }
+}
+

+    std::printf("Options:\n");

+    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
+    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
 }
 
+bool parseSize(const char* text, size_t& value) noexcept {
+    errno = 0;
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long parsed = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (errno != 0 || end == text || *end != '\0' || parsed == 0 ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max()) ||
+        parsed > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max())) {
+        return false;
+    }
+    value = static_cast<size_t>(parsed);
+    return value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / value &&
+           value * value <= std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double);
+}
